correcting for the video offset and aligning to the go cue, in seconds\n   294→    def _frame_time(self, trial, view):\n   295→        \"\"\"Frame times of one trial and camera, in seconds from go cue onset.\"\"\"\n   296→        frame_times = np.ravel(self._traj(trial, view)['frameTimes'])\n   297→        return frame_times - self.offset - self.go_cue[trial]\n   298→\n   299→    def _camera_of(self, trial, feature):\n   300→        \"\"\"The camera that tracks a feature, and its tracking for this trial.\"\"\"\n   301→        for view in (SIDE, BOTTOM):\n   302→            traj = self._traj(trial, view)\n   303→            if feature in list(traj['featNames']):     # the name picks out the camera\n   304→                return view, traj\n   305→        raise KeyError('%s is tracked by neither camera' % feature)\n   306→\n   307→    # x, y, and likelihood within the time window\n   308→    def _track(self, trial, feature):\n   309→        \"\"\"x, y, and likelihood of one feature over the frames inside the window.\"\"\"\n   310→        view, traj = self._camera_of(trial, feature)\n   311→        x, y, likelihood = _feature(traj, list(traj['featNames']).index(feature))\n   312→        time = self._frame_time(trial, view)           # frame counts can differ between views\n   313→        inside = (time >= T_START) & (time <= T_STOP)\n   314→        return time[inside], x[inside], y[inside], likelihood[inside]\n   315→\n   316→    # smooth the x, y postion, and compute the speed, filter by the likelihood threshold > 0.90\n   317→    def _velocity(self, track):\n   318→        \"\"\"Combined velocity over the valid frames, NaN elsewhere.\"\"\"\n   319→        time, x, y, likelihood = track\n   320→        speed = np.full(time.size, np.nan)\n   321→        if time.size < 2:                              # a few trials have no frame times\n   322→            return time, speed\n   323→\n   324→        sigma = SMOOTH_SIGMA_MS / 1000 / np.median(np.diff(time))\n   325→        valid = likelihood > LIKELIHOOD_CUT            # x and y are NaN below this\n   326→\n   327→        # smooth and differentiate inside each run of tracked frames, never across a gap\n   328→        for start, stop in _runs(valid):\n   329→            if stop - start < 2:                       # a lone frame has no velocity\n   330→                continue\n   331→            sx = gaussian_filter1d(x[start:stop], sigma, mode='mirror')\n   332→            sy = gaussian_filter1d(y[start:stop], sigma, mode='mirror')\n   333→            speed[start:stop] = np.hypot(np.gradient(sx, time[start:stop]),\n   334→                                         np.gradient(sy, time[start:stop]))\n   335→        return time, speed\n   336→\n   337→    def _view_scale(self, velocity):\n   338→        \"\"\"The percentile that puts one camera's velocity on a comparable scale.\"\"\"\n   339→        pooled = np.concatenate([velocity[trial][1] for trial in self.trials])\n   340→        return np.nanpercentile(pooled, NORMALIZE_PCT)\n   341→\n   342→    # the tongue is seen by both cameras, on different pixel scales, so each view is\n   343→    # binned and divided by its own percentile before the two are averaged\n   344→    def tongue_velocity(self):\n   345→        \"\"\"Binned tongue velocity, as (n_trials, N_BINS).\"\"\"\n   346→        side = np.array([_bin_frames(*self.side_tongue[trial]) for trial in self.trials])\n   347→        bottom = np.array([_bin_frames(*self.bottom_tongue[trial]) for trial in self.trials])\n   348→        side = side / self.side_scale\n   349→        bottom = bottom / self.bottom_scale\n   350→        return _mean_over_available(side, bottom)      # one view alone where the other is missing\n   351→\n   352→    # only one paw stays tracked through the delay, so it is used on its own, in pixels\n   353→    def paw_velocity(self):\n   354→        \"\"\"Binned paw velocity, as (n_trials, N_BINS).\"\"\"\n   355→        return np.array([_bin_frames(*self.paw[trial]) for trial in self.trials])\n   356→\n   357→    # motion energy is already one number per frame, so it only needs binning\n   358→    def motion_energy(self):\n   359→        \"\"\"Binned motion energy, as (n_trials, N_BINS).\"\"\"\n   360→        rows = []\n   361→        for trial in self.trials:\n   362→            time = self._frame_time(trial, SIDE)       # motion energy follows the side camera\n   363→            inside = (time >= T_START) & (time <= T_STOP)\n   364→            rows.append(_bin_frames(time[inside], self.energy[trial][inside]))\n   365→        return np.array(rows)\n   366→\n   367→    def outputs(self):\n   368→        \"\"\"The three streams, each cut in two at its own session median.\"\"\"\n   369→        tongue = self.tongue_velocity()\n   370→        paw = self.paw_velocity()\n   371→        energy = self.motion_energy()\n   372→        return (_discretize(tongue, np.nanpercentile(tongue, SPLIT_PCT)),\n   373→                _discretize(paw, np.nanpercentile(paw, SPLIT_PCT)),\n   374→                _discretize(energy, np.nanpercentile(energy, SPLIT_PCT)))\n   375→\n   376→\n   377→# ------------------------------------------------------------------- neural --\n   378→# Spike times are already on the behavior clock and relative to trial start, so aligning\n   379→# to the go cue is one subtraction. Quality is a free text label written with either case\n   380→# and with a few typos, so it is matched in lower case.\n   381→\n   382→def _quality(cluster):\n   383→    \"\"\"Quality label of a cluster; a handful carry no label at all.\"\"\"\n   384→    label = cluster['quality']\n   385→    return label.strip().lower() if isinstance(label, str) else ''\n   386→\n   387→\n   388→class Neural:\n   389→    \"\"\"The QC-passing units of a session, as smoothed firing rates.\"\"\"\n   390→\n   391→    def __init__(self, obj, probes, trials):\n   392→        self.obj = obj\n   393→        self.go_cue = trial_column(obj['bp'], 'ev', 'goCue')\n   394→        self.clusters = [cluster for probe in probes                # probes concatenated\n   395→                         for cluster in self._probe_clusters(probe)\n   396→                         if _quality(cluster) not in QUALITY_DROP]\n   397→\n   398→        # two sessions keep running after the recording stops, leaving trials with no spikes\n   399→        last = max(int(np.asarray(cluster['trial'], int).max(initial=0))\n   400→                   for cluster in self.clusters)\n   401→        self.trials = np.asarray([t for t in trials if t < last])   # trial numbers are 1 based\n   402→\n   403→    def _probe_clusters(self, probe):\n   404→        \"\"\"One dict per cluster on the given probe, however the file stores them.\"\"\"\n   405→        entry = self.obj['clu'][probe - 1]\n   406→        if isinstance(entry, dict) and isinstance(entry['tm'], list):\n   407→            # v7.3: one entry per probe, each field holding one array per cluster\n   408→            return [{field: entry[field][i] for field in entry}\n   409→                    for i in range(len(entry['tm']))]\n   410→        return self.obj['clu']                     # v5: one entry per cluster, single probe\n   411→\n   412→    def spike_count(self, cluster):\n   413→        \"\"\"Spikes of one cluster per trial and bin, aligned to the go cue.\"\"\"\n   414→        spike_trial = np.asarray(cluster['trial'], int) - 1         # trial numbers are 1 based\n   415→        spike_time = np.asarray(cluster['trialtm'], float) - self.go_cue[spike_trial]\n   416→\n   417→        # count spikes into a trial by bin grid; those outside the window fall off the edges\n   418→        trial_edges = np.arange(self.go_cue.size + 1) - 0.5\n   419→        counts, _, _ = np.histogram2d(spike_trial, spike_time, bins=[trial_edges, BIN_EDGES])\n   420→        return counts[self.trials]                                  # only the trials we keep\n   421→\n   422→    def rates(self):\n   423→        \"\"\"Firing rate of every unit above MIN_RATE, as (n_units, n_trials, N_BINS).\"\"\"\n   424→        sigma = RATE_SD_MS / 1000 / BIN\n   425→        rates = []\n   426→        for cluster in self.clusters:\n   427→            rate = self.spike_count(cluster) / BIN                 # (n_trials, 1, N_BINS)\n   457→\n   458→    return {\n   459→        'session_id': name,\n   460→        'subject': name.split('_')[0],\n   461→        'neural': [np.ascontiguousarray(rates[:, t, :]) for t in range(n_trials)],\n   462→        'input': [inp[t] for t in range(n_trials)],\n   463→        'output': [out[t] for t in range(n_trials)],\n   464→        'n_trials': n_trials,\n   465→        'n_units': int(rates.shape[0]),\n   466→    }\n   467→\n   468→\n   469→def assemble(results):\n   470→    \"\"\"Stitch per-session results into the target dictionary.\"\"\"\n   471→    subjects = sorted({r['subject'] for r in results})\n   472→    sub_ix = {s: i for i, s in enumerate(subjects)}\n   473→\n   474→    return {\n   475→        'neural': [r['neural'] for r in results],\n   476→        'input': [r['input'] for r in results],\n   477→        'output': [r['output'] for r in results],\n   478→\n   479→        'subjects': subjects,\n   480→        'subject_idx': np.array([sub_ix[r['subject']] for r in results], np.int32),\n   481→\n   482→        'brain_regions': [BRAIN_REGION],\n   483→        'brain_region_idx': [np.zeros(r['n_units'], np.int32) for r in results],\n   484→\n   485→        'input_names': INPUT_NAMES,\n   486→        'output_names': OUTPUT_NAMES,\n   487→        'output_values': OUTPUT_VALUES,\n   488→\n   489→        'metadata': {\n   490→            'task_description':\n   491→                'Head-fixed mice lick left or right for water. struct\n        return {k: _h5(f, node[k]) for k in node}\n    if node.attrs.get('MATLAB_class', b'').decode() == 'char':\n        return ''.join(chr(c) for c in np.asarray(node[()]).ravel())\n    if node.dtype == object:                                # Early-lick and photostim trials\n# are dropped, following the paper. The index keeps the original trial numbers, which\n# every later step uses to reach back into the raw arrays.\n\ndef trial_column(bp, *names):\n    \"\"\"One entry per trial from a field of bp, e.g. trial_column(bp, 'stim', 'enable').\"\"\"\n    n_trials = int(np.ravel(bp['Ntrials'])[0])\n    field = bp\n    for name in names:\n        field = field[name]\n    return np.ravel(np.asarray(field, float))[:n_trials]\n\n\ndef trial_info(bp):\n    \"\"\"Outcome, context, and lick direction per trial, without early-lick or photostim trials.\"\"\"\n    hit = trial_column(bp, 'hit') > 0\n    miss = trial_column(bp, 'miss') > 0\n    right = trial_column(bp, 'R') > 0\n    autowater = trial_column(bp, 'autowater') > 0\n    early = trial_column(bp, 'early') > 0\n    stim = trial_column(bp, 'stim', 'enable') > 0\n    n_trials = hit.size\n\n    # a hit is a correct lick and a miss an incorrect one; the animal ignored the rest\n    outcome = np.full(n_trials, OUTCOME['ignore'])\n    outcome[hit] = OUTCOME['correct']\n    outcome[miss] = OUTCOME['incorrect']\n\n    # water is given from a random port without any cue in the WC context\n    context = np.where(autowater, CONTEXT['WC'], CONTEXT['DR'])\n\n    # a hit licks the instructed port, a miss licks the other one, an ignore neither\n    lick_direction = np.full(n_trials, LICK['no lick'])\n    lick_direction[hit] = np.where(right[hit], LICK['right'], LICK['left'])\n    lick_direction[miss] = np.where(right[miss], LICK['left'], LICK['right'])\n\n    info = pd.DataFrame({'lick_direction': lick_direction, 'context': context,\n                         'outcome': outcome})\n    info.index.name = 'trial'\n    return info[~early & ~stim]\n\n\n# ------------------------------------------------------------------- camera --\n# Tongue velocity, paw velocity, and motion energy for one session. A class because the\n# video offset, the per-view scales, and the median split are session-wide\n# quantities that every trial reuses.\n\ndef _feature(traj, index):\n    \"\"\"x, y, and likelihood of one feature, whichever way the file orders ts.\"\"\"\n    ts = np.asarray(traj['ts'])\n    if ts.shape[0] == len(traj['featNames']):      # v7.3 reads it as (features, xyl, frames)\n        return ts[index]\n    return ts[:, :, index].T                       # v5 keeps MATLAB's (frames, xyl, features)\n\n\ndef _runs(valid):\n    \"\"\"First and last+1 index of each contiguous run of valid frames.\"\"\"\n    edges = np.diff(np.concatenate([[0], valid.astype(int), [0]]))\n    return zip(np.flatnonzero(edges == 1), np.flatnonzero(edges == -1))\n\n\ndef _bin_frames(time, values):\n    \"\"\"Mean of the valid frames in each bin, NaN where a bin holds no valid frame.\"\"\"\n    bin_of_frame = np.searchsorted(BIN_EDGES, time, side='right') - 1\n    valid = np.isfinite(values) & (bin_of_frame >= 0) & (bin_of_frame < N_BINS)\n    total = np.bincount(bin_of_frame[valid], weights=values[valid], minlength=N_BINS)\n    count = np.bincount(bin_of_frame[valid], minlength=N_BINS)\n    return np.where(count > 0, total / np.maximum(count, 1), np.nan)\n\n\ndef _mean_over_available(first, second):\n    \"\"\"Mean of the two, whichever one is there alone, or NaN where neither is.\"\"\"\n    stacked = np.stack([first, second])\n    count = np.sum(np.isfinite(stacked), axis=0)\n    total = np.nansum(stacked, axis=0)\n    return np.where(count > 0, total / np.maximum(count, 1), np.nan)\n\n\ndef _discretize(values, threshold):\n    \"\"\"Two classes split at the threshold; untracked bins take the trailing class.\"\"\"\n    code = np.where(values >= threshold, MOVEMENT['above threshold'], MOVEMENT['below threshold'])\n    return np.where(np.isnan(values), MOVEMENT['not visible'], code)\n\n\nclass Camera:\n    \"\"\"The three camera streams of a session, binned and cut into classes.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, obj, name, trials, datadir):\n        self.obj = obj\n        self.trials = list(trials)\n        self.go_cue = trial_column(obj['bp'], 'ev', 'goCue')\n        self.offset = self._video_offset()             # one constant for the whole session\n        self.energy = load_motion_energy(name, datadir)\n\n        # frame resolution velocity, for the two tongue views and for the paw\n        self.side_tongue = self._trial_velocity(SIDE_TONGUE)\n        self.bottom_tongue = self._trial_velocity(BOTTOM_TONGUE)\n        self.paw = self._trial_velocity(PAW)\n\n        # the two cameras see the tongue at different pixel scales\n        self.side_scale = self._view_scale(self.side_tongue)\n        self.bottom_scale = self._view_scale(self.bottom_tongue)\n\n    def _trial_velocity(self, feature):\n        \"\"\"Frame resolution velocity of one tracked feature, one entry per trial.\"\"\"\n        return {trial: self._velocity(self._track(trial, feature)) for trial in self.trials}\n\n    def _video_offset(self):\n        \"\"\"Seconds by which the video clock leads the behavior clock (findVideoOffset.m).\"\"\"\n        sample_rate = float(np.ravel(self.obj['sglx']['fs'])[0])\n        video_start = np.ravel(self.obj['sglx']['bitcode']['bitstart']) / sample_rate\n        behavior_start = trial_column(self.obj['bp'], 'ev', 'bitStart')\n        return pd.Series(video_start).mode().iloc[0] - pd.Series(behavior_start).mode().iloc[0]\n\n    def _traj(self, trial, view):\n        \"\"\"Tracking of one trial and camera, however the file stores it.\"\"\"\n        traj = self.obj['traj'][view]\n        if isinstance(traj, dict):                     # v7.3: one entry per camera\n            return {field: traj[field][trial] for field in traj}\n        entry = traj[trial]                            # v5: one entry per trial\n        if hasattr(entry, '_fieldnames'):              # scipy leaves nested structs as objects\n            return {field: getattr(entry, field) for field in entry._fieldnames}\n        return entry\n\n    # camera frame time after correcting for the video offset and aligning to the go cue, in seconds\n    def _frame_time(self, trial, view):\n        \"\"\"Frame times of one trial and camera, in seconds from go cue onset.\"\"\"\n        frame_times = np.ravel(self._traj(trial, view)['frameTimes'])\n        return frame_times - self.offset - self.go_cue[trial]\n\n    def _camera_of(self, trial, feature):\n        \"\"\"The camera that tracks a feature, and its tracking for this trial.\"\"\"\n        for view in (SIDE, BOTTOM):\n            traj = self._traj(trial, view)\n            if feature in list(traj['featNames']):     # the name picks out the camera\n                return view, traj\n        raise KeyError('%s is tracked by neither camera' % feature)\n\n    # x, y, and likelihood within the time window\n    def _track(self, trial, feature):\n        \"\"\"x, y, and likelihood of one feature over the frames inside the window.\"\"\"\n        view, traj = self._camera_of(trial, feature)\n        x, y, likelihood = _feature(traj, list(traj['featNames']).index(feature))\n        time = self._frame_time(trial, view)           # frame counts can differ between views\n        inside = (time >= T_START) & (time <= T_STOP)\n        return time[inside], x[inside], y[inside], likelihood[inside]\n\n    # smooth the x, y postion, and compute the speed, filter by the likelihood threshold > 0.90\n    def _velocity(self, track):\n        \"\"\"Combined velocity over the valid frames, NaN elsewhere.\"\"\"\n        time, x, y, likelihood = track\n        speed = np.full(time.size, np.nan)\n        if time.size < 2:                              # a few trials have no frame times\n            return time, speed\n\n        sigma = SMOOTH_SIGMA_MS / 1000 / np.median(np.diff(time))\n        valid = likelihood > LIKELIHOOD_CUT            # x and y are NaN below this\n\n        # smooth and differentiate inside each run of tracked frames, never across a gap\n        for start, stop in _runs(valid):\n            if stop - start < 2:                       # a lone frame has no velocity\n                continue\n            sx = gaussian_filter1d(x[start:stop], sigma, mode='mirror')\n            sy = gaussian_filter1d(y[start:stop], sigma, mode='mirror')\n            speed[start:stop] = np.hypot(np.gradient(sx, time[start:stop]),\n                                         np.gradient(sy, time[start:stop]))\n        return time, speed\n\n    def _view_scale(self, velocity):\n        \"\"\"The percentile that puts one camera's velocity on a comparable scale.\"\"\"\n        pooled = np.concatenate([velocity[trial][1] for trial in self.trials])\n        return np.nanpercentile(pooled, NORMALIZE_PCT)\n\n    # the tongue is seen by both cameras, on different pixel scales, so each view is\n    # binned and divided by its own percentile before the two are averaged\n    def tongue_velocity(self):\n        \"\"\"Binned tongue velocity, as (n_trials, N_BINS).\"\"\"\n        side = np.array([_bin_frames(*self.side_tongue[trial]) for trial in self.trials])\n        bottom = np.array([_bin_frames(*self.bottom_tongue[trial]) for trial in self.trials])\n        side = side / self.side_scale\n        bottom = bottom / self.bottom_scale\n        return _mean_over_available(side, bottom)      # one view alone where the other is missing\n\n    # only one paw stays tracked through the delay, so it is used on its own, in pixels\n    def paw_velocity(self):\n        \"\"\"Binned paw velocity, as (n_trials, N_BINS).\"\"\"\n        return np.array([_bin_frames(*self.paw[trial]) for trial in self.trials])\n\n    # motion energy is already one number per frame, so it only needs binning\n    def motion_energy(self):\n        \"\"\"Binned motion energy, as (n_trials, N_BINS).\"\"\"\n        rows = []\n        for trial in self.trials:\n            time = self._frame_time(trial, SIDE)       # motion energy follows the side camera\n            inside = (time >= T_START) & (time <= T_STOP)\n            rows.append(_bin_frames(time[inside], self.energy[trial][inside]))\n        return np.array(rows)\n\n    def outputs(self):\n        \"\"\"The three streams, each cut in two at its own session median.\"\"\"\n        tongue = self.tongue_velocity()\n        paw = self.paw_velocity()\n        energy = self.motion_energy()\n        return (_discretize(tongue, np.nanpercentile(tongue, SPLIT_PCT)),\n                _discretize(paw, np.nanpercentile(paw, SPLIT_PCT)),\n                _discretize(energy, np.nanpercentile(energy, SPLIT_PCT)))\n\n\n# ------------------------------------------------------------------- neural --\n# Spike times are already on the behavior clock and relative to trial start, so aligning\n# to the go cue is one subtraction. Quality is a free text label written with either case\n# and with a few typos, so it is matched in lower case.\n\ndef _quality(cluster):\n    \"\"\"Quality label of a cluster; a handful carry no label at all.\"\"\"\n    label = cluster['quality']\n    return label.strip().lower() if isinstance(label, str) else ''\n\n\nclass Neural:\n    \"\"\"The QC-passing units of a session, as smoothed firing rates.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, obj, probes, trials):\n        self.obj = obj\n        self.go_cue = trial_column(obj['bp'], 'ev', 'goCue')\n        self.clusters = [cluster for probe in probes                # probes concatenated\n                         for cluster in self._probe_clusters(probe)\n                         if _quality(cluster) not in QUALITY_DROP]\n\n        # two sessions keep running after the recording stops, leaving trials with no spikes\n        last = max(int(np.asarray(cluster['trial'], int).max(initial=0))\n                   for cluster in self.clusters)\n        self.trials = np.asarray([t for t in trials if t < last])   # trial numbers are 1 based\n\n    def _probe_clusters(self, probe):\n        \"\"\"One dict per cluster on the given probe, however the file stores them.\"\"\"\n        entry = self.obj['clu'][probe - 1]\n        if isinstance(entry, dict) and isinstance(entry['tm'], list):\n            # v7.3: one entry per probe, each field holding one array per cluster\n            return [{field: entry[field][i] for field in entry}\n                    for i in range(len(entry['tm']))]\n        return self.obj['clu']                     # v5: one entry per cluster, single probe\n\n    def spike_count(self, cluster):\n        \"\"\"Spikes of one cluster per trial and bin, aligned to the go cue.\"\"\"\n        spike_trial = np.asarray(cluster['trial'], int) - 1         # trial numbers are 1 based\n        spike_time = np.asarray(cluster['trialtm'], float) - self.go_cue[spike_trial]\n\n        # count spikes into a trial by bin grid; those outside the window fall off the edges\n        trial_edges = np.arange(self.go_cue.size + 1) - 0.5\n        counts, _, _ = np.histogram2d(spike_trial, spike_time, bins=[trial_edges, BIN_EDGES])\n        return counts[self.trials]                                  # only the trials we keep\n\n    def rates(self):\n        \"\"\"Firing rate of every unit above MIN_RATE, as (n_units, n_trials, N_BINS).\"\"\"\n        sigma = RATE_SD_MS / 1000 / BIN\n        rates = []\n        for cluster in self.clusters:\n            rate = self.spike_count(cluster) / BIN                 # counts -> Hz\n            if rate.mean() > MIN_RATE:\n                rates.append(gaussian_filter1d(rate, sigma, axis=1, mode='reflect'))\n        return np.asarray(rates, np.float32)\n\n\n# ------------------------------------------------------------------ session --\n\ndef process_session(name, datadir):\n    \"\"\"Convert one session into per-trial neural, input, and output arrays.\"\"\"\n    obj = load_mat(session_file(name, 'data_structure', datadir))\n    info = trial_info(obj['bp'])\n\n    neural = Neural(obj, SESSIONS[name], info.index)\n    info = info.loc[neural.trials]                 # drops any trial past the recording\n    n_trials = len(info)\n\n    rates = neural.rates()\n    tongue, paw, energy = Camera(obj, name, info.index, datadir).outputs()\n\n    # the three per-trial values are repeated across bins so all six outputs share one array\n    out = np.empty((n_trials, len(OUTPUT_NAMES), N_BINS), np.int8)\n    out[:, 0, :] = info['lick_direction'].to_numpy()[:, None]\n    out[:, 1, :] = info['context'].to_numpy()[:, None]\n    out[:, 2, :] = info['outcome'].to_numpy()[:, None]\n    out[:, 3, :] = tongue\n    out[:, 4, :] = paw\n    out[:, 5, :] = energy\n\n    inp = np.tile(TIME.astype(np.float32), (n_trials, 1, 1))       # (n_trials, 1, N_BINS)\n\n    return {\n        'session_id': name,\n        'subject': name.split('_')[0],\n        'neural': [np.ascontiguousarray(rates[:, t, :]) for t in range(n_trials)],\n        'input': [inp[t] for t in range(n_trials)],\n        'output': [out[t] for t in range(n_trials)],\n        'n_trials': n_trials,\n        'n_units': int(rates.shape[0]),\n    }\n\n\ndef assemble(results):\n    \"\"\"Stitch per-session results into the target dictionary.\"\"\"\n    subjects = sorted({r['subject'] for r in results})\n    sub_ix = {s: i for i, s in enumerate(subjects)}\n\n    return {\n        'neural': [r['neural'] for r in results],\n        'input': [r['input'] for r in results],\n        'output': [r['output'] for r in results],\n\n        'subjects': subjects,\n        'subject_idx': np.array([sub_ix[r['subject']] for r in results], np.int32),\n\n        'brain_regions': [BRAIN_REGION],\n        'brain_region_idx': [np.zeros(r['n_units'], np.int32) for r in results],\n\n        'input_names': INPUT_NAMES,\n        'output_names': OUTPUT_NAMES,\n        'output_values': OUTPUT_VALUES,\n\n        'metadata': {\n            'task_description':\n                'Head-fixed mice lick left or right for water. In the delayed-response '\n                'context a tone instructs the direction, a delay follows, and an auditory '\n                'go cue opens the response window; in the water-cued context all cues are '\n                'omitted and water arrives at a random port. Decoded variables: lick '\n                'direction, behavioral context, outcome, and tongue, paw, and whole-body '\n                'movement.',\n            'time_bin_size': BIN * 1000.0,          # ms\n            'temporal_alignment_event': 'go cue onset (auditory chirp, 10 ms)',\n            'off_start': T_START,\n            'off_end': T_STOP,\n\n            'dataset': 'Hasnain, Birnbaum et al., Nat Neurosci 2024, ALM recordings',\n            'neural_units': 'Hz',\n            'session_selection':\n                \"The 44 sessions listed in the author's DataLoadingScripts/Recording and \"\n                'video/load<ANM>_ALMVideo.m that are present in data/, with the probe each '\n                'entry specifies; two-probe sessions are concatenated.',\n            'unit_curation':\n                'Cluster quality labels, lower cased, excluding %s, then units whose mean '\n                'rate over the window is at or below %.1f Hz.'\n                % (', '.join(sorted(QUALITY_DROP)), MIN_RATE),\n            'trial_curation': 'Early-lick and photostimulation trials excluded.',\n            'neural_processing':\n                'Spikes counted in %.0f ms bins, converted to Hz, smoothed with a gaussian '\n                'of %d ms standard deviation.' % (BIN * 1000, RATE_SD_MS),\n            'movement_discretisation':\n                'Tongue and paw velocity are the speed of the DeepLabCut position after a '\n                '%d ms gaussian, computed within each run of frames whose likelihood '\n                'exceeds %.1f. The two tongue views are each divided by their %dth '\n                'percentile before being averaged. Each stream is averaged into %.0f ms '\n                'bins and split at its own session %dth percentile; bins the camera did '\n                'not track take the trailing \"not visible\" class.'\n                % (SMOOTH_SIGMA_MS, LIKELIHOOD_CUT, NORMALIZE_PCT, BIN * 1000, SPLIT_PCT),\n            'session_info': [\n                {'session_id': r['session_id'], 'subject': r['subject'],\n                 'n_trials': r['n_trials'], 'n_units': r['n_units']}\n                for r in results\n            ],\n        },\n    }\n\n\ndef main():\n    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)\n    ap.add_argument('outfile', help='output pickle path')\n    ap.add_argument('--full', action='store_true', help='process all sessions (default)')\n    ap.add_argument('--sample', action='store_true', help='process only 2 sessions')\n    ap.add_argument('--datadir', default=DATA_DIR,\n                    help='directory holding the Ephys_Behavior/ folders (default: %(default)s)')\n    args = ap.parse_args()\n\n    names = SAMPLE if args.sample else list(SESSIONS)\n    print('%d sessions' % len(names), flush=True)\n\n    t0 = time.time()\n    results = []\n    for i, name in enumerate(names, 1):\n        t1 = time.time()\n        res = process_session(name, args.datadir)\n        results.append(res)\n        elapsed = time.time() - t0\n        print('[%2d/%d] %-18s %4d trials %4d units  %5.1fs  (elapsed %5.1fs, total ~%5.1fs)'\n              % (i, len(names), name, res['n_trials'], res['n_units'],\n                 time.time() - t1, elapsed, elapsed / i * len(names)), flush=True)\n\n    data = assemble(results)\n    print('\\nconverted in %.1f s' % (time.time() - t0), flush=True)\n    print('sessions %d | subjects %d | trials %d | units %d'\n          % (len(results), len(data['subjects']),\n             sum(r['n_trials'] for r in results), sum(r['n_units'] for r in results)),\n          flush=True)\n\n    with open(args.outfile, 'wb') as f:\n        pickle.dump(data, f, protocol=pickle.HIGHEST_PROTOCOL)\n    print('wrote %s (%.1f GB)'\n          % (args.outfile, os.path.getsize(args.outfile) / 1e9), flush=True)\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n","numLines":571,"startLine":1,"totalLines":571}}}

282→\n   283→def load_motion_energy(fn):\n   284→    \"\"\"Load motionEnergy_ANM_DATE.mat.\n   285→\n   286→    Three layouts occur in the released data (all handled by loadMotionEnergy.m, which does\n   287→    `me = temp.me; if isstruct(me.data), me.data = me.data.data; end`):\n   288→      1. `me` struct with fields {data (cell of per-trial 400 Hz vectors), moveThresh}\n   289→      2. `me` struct whose `data` field is itself a struct with {data, moveThresh}\n   290→      3. `me` saved directly as the cell array of per-trial vectors (no moveThresh)\n   291→    \"\"\"\n   292→    m = sio.loadmat(fn, struct_as_record=False, squeeze_me=True)\n   293→    me = m['me']\n   294→    thresh = np.nan\n   295→    raw = me\n   296→    # descend through nested structs until we reach the cell array of trials\n   297→    while hasattr(raw, '_fieldnames'):\n   298→        if 'moveThresh' in raw._fieldnames:\n   299→            try:\n   300→                thresh = float(np.array(raw.moveThresh).ravel()[0])\n   301→            except Exception:\n   302→                pass\n   303→        if 'data' not in raw._fieldnames:\n   304→            break\n   305→        raw = raw.data\n   306→    data = [np.atleast_1d(np.array(d).ravel()).astype(float) for d in np.atleast_1d(raw)]\n   307→    return dict(data=data, moveThresh=thresh)\n   308→\n   309→# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   310→# Reference processing helpers\n   311→# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   312→def gausswin(N, alpha=2.5):\n   313→    \"\"\"MATLAB gausswin(N, alpha) (default alpha = 2.5).\"\"\"\n   314→    n = np.arange(N) - (N - 1) / 2.0\n   315→    return np.exp(-0.5 * (alpha * n / ((N - 1) / 2.0)) ** 2)\n   316→\n   317→def causal_kernel(N=SMOOTH_N):\n   318→    \"\"\"Kernel used by utils/mySmooth.m: gausswin with the first floor(N/2) taps zeroed.\"\"\"\n   319→    k = gausswin(N)\n   320→    k[:N // 2] = 0.0\n   321→    return k / k.sum()\n   322→\n   323→KERN = causal_kernel()\n   324→\n   325→def my_smooth(x, kern=KERN, bctype=BCTYPE):\n   326→    \"\"\"utils/mySmooth.m, operating on the LAST axis (time).\n   327→\n   328→    MATLAB: x_filt = cat(1, x(1:N,:), x); conv(...,'same'); out = out(N+1:end,:)\n   329→    \"\"\"\n   330→    from scipy.signal import fftconvolve\n   331→    N = len(kern)\n   332→    if bctype == 'reflect':\n   333→        xf = np.concatenate([x[..., :N], x], axis=-1)\n   334→        trim = N\n   335→    else:\n   336→        xf = x\n   337→        trim = 0\n   338→    out = fftconvolve(xf, kern.reshape((1,) * (xf.ndim - 1) + (N,)), mode='same', axes=-1)\n   339→    return out[..., trim:]\n   340→\n   341→def time_axes():\n   342→    \"\"\"Fine (10 ms) bin edges/centres and the down-sampled decoder bin centres.\"\"\"\n   343→    edges = np.round(np.arange(TMIN, TMAX + 1e-9, DT_FINE), 6)\n   344→    centres = edges[:-1] + DT_FINE / 2.0\n   345→    nfine = len(centres)\n   346→    nout = nfine // DOWNSAMPLE\n   347→    centres_out = centres[:nout * DOWNSAMPLE].reshape(nout, DOWNSAMPLE).mean(axis=1)\n   348→    return edges, centres, centres_out\n   349→\n   350→EDGES, TCENT, TOUT = time_axes()\n   351→NFINE, NOUT = len(TCENT), len(TOUT)\n   352→\n   353→def downsample_mean(x, k=DOWNSAMPLE, nanmean=False):\n   354→    \"\"\"Average k consecutive samples along the last axis.\"\"\"\n   355→    n = (x.shape[-1] // k) * k\n   356→    y = x[..., :n].reshape(x.shape[:-1] + (n // k, k))\n   357→    if nanmean:\n   358→        with np.errstate(invalid='ignore'):\n   359→            return np.nanmean(y, axis=-1)\n   360→    return y.mean(axis=-1)\n   361→\n   362→def video_offset(sess):\n   363→    \"\"\"funcs/findVideoOffset.m: mode(sglx.bitcode.bitstart)/fs - mode(bp.ev.bitStart).\"\"\"\n   364→    from scipy.stats import mode as smode\n   365→    bs = sess['sglx']['bitstart']\n   366→    bs = bs[~np.isnan(bs)]\n   367→    bstart = sess['bp']['ev']['bitStart']\n   368→    bstart = bstart[~np.isnan(bstart)]\n   369→    if bs.size == 0 or bstart.size == 0:\n   370→        return 0.5\n   371→    vid_file_offset = float(smode(bs, keepdims=False).mode) / sess['sglx']['fs']\n   372→    return vid_file_offset - float(smode(np.round(bstart, 6), keepdims=False).mode)\n   373→\n   374→def frame_times_for_trial(sess, trial, vidshift, align_t):\n   375→    \"\"\"Frame times relative to the alignment event (reference fallback if frameTimes bad).\"\"\"\n   376→    ft = sess['frameTimes'][trial]\n   377→    if ft.size == 0 or np.all(np.isnan(ft)):\n   378→        ft = (np.arange(1, sess['nframes'][trial] + 1) / VIDEO_FS)\n   379→        return ft - 0.5 - align_t\n   380→    return ft - vidshift - align_t\n   381→\n   382→# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   383→# Neural data\n   384→# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   385→def bin_spikes(clu, probes, align_times, keep_trials, ntrials):\n   386→    \"\"\"Bin + smooth spikes exactly like DataLoadingScripts/getSeq.m + alignSpikes.m.\n   387→\n   388→    Returns (rates (ntrials_kept, nunits, NOUT) in spikes/s, list of (probe, cluster idx)).\n   389→    \"\"\"\n   390→    rates, ids = [], []\n   391→    keep_idx = {t: i for i, t in enumerate(keep_trials)}   # 0-based trial -> row\n   392→    nkeep = len(keep_trials)\n   393→    for p in probes:\n   394→        c = clu.get(p)\n   395→        if c is None:\n   396→            continue\n   397→        good = [i for i, q in enumerate(c['quality'])\n   398→                if q.strip().lower() not in BAD_QUALITY]          # findClusters.m\n   399→        for i in good:\n   400→            tr = c['trial'][i].astype(np.int64) - 1               # -> 0-based\n   401→            tm = c['trialtm'][i]\n   402→            ok = np.array([t in keep_idx for t in tr]) if tr.size else np.zeros(0, bool)\n   403→            if tr.size:\n   404→                row = np.full(tr.shape, -1, dtype=np.int64)\n   405→                for j in np.nonzero(ok)[0]:\n   406→                    row[j] = keep_idx[tr[j]]\n   407→            else:\n   408→                row = np.zeros(0, dtype=np.int64)\n   409→            counts = np.zeros((nkeep, NFINE), dtype=np.float32)\n   410→            if tr.size:\n   411→                sel = ok & np.isfinite(tm)\n   412→                if sel.any():\n   413→                    aligned = tm[sel] - align_times[tr[sel]]\n   414→                    b = np.floor((aligned - TMIN) / DT_FINE).astype(np.int64)\n   415→                    inwin = (b >= 0) & (b < NFINE)\n   416→                    if inwin.any():\n   417→                        flat = row[sel][inwin] * NFINE + b[inwin]\n   418→                        np.add.at(counts.reshape(-1), flat, 1.0)\n   419→            rate = counts / DT_FINE                                # spikes / s\n   420→            rate = my_smooth(rate)                                 # causal gaussian\n   421→            rates.append(downsample_mean(rate).astype(np.float32))\n   422→            ids.append((p, i))\n   423→    if not rates:\n   424→        return np.zeros((nkeep, 0, NOUT), np.float32), []\n   425→    return np.stack(rates, axis=1), ids\n   426→\n   427→# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   428→# Kinematics / motion energy\n   429→# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   430→def feature_speed(sess, key, keep_trials, vidshift, align_times, fill_missing):\n   431→    \"\"\"Speed of a DLC feature on the fine time axis, NaN where the feature is not visible.\n   432→\n   433→    Mirrors funcs/kinematics/findPosition.m + findVelocity.m:\n   434→      * interp1(frameTimes - vidshift - alignEvent, [x y], taxis)\n   435→      * tongue: no smoothing, NaNs kept (not visible)\n   436→      * other features: nearest-fill + baseline-derivative subtraction\n   437→    \"\"\"\n   438→    tr = sess['traj'].get(key)\n   439→    nkeep = len(keep_trials)\n   440→    speed = np.full((nkeep, NFINE), np.nan)\n   441→    visible = np.zeros((nkeep, NFINE), bool)\n   442→    if tr is None:\n   443→        return speed, visible\n   444→    nd = tr['NdroppedFrames']\n   445→    for r, t in enumerate(keep_trials):\n   446→        if t >= len(tr['xy']):\n   447→            continue\n   448→        if t < len(nd) and np.isnan(nd[t]):      # reference skips these trials\n   449→            continue\n   450→        ft = frame_times_for_trial(sess, t, vidshift, align_times[t])\n   451→        xy = tr['xy'][t]\n   452→        if ft.size != xy.shape[0] or ft.size < 2:\n   453→            m = min(ft.size, xy.shape[0])\n   454→            if m < 2:\n   455→                continue\n   456→            ft, xy = ft[:m], xy[:m]\n   457→        valid = np.isfinite(ft)\n   458→        if valid.sum() < 2:\n   459→            continue\n   460→        pos = np.empty((NFINE, 2))\n   461→        for d in range(2):\n   462→            pos[:, d] = np.interp(TCENT, ft[valid], xy[valid, d], left=np.nan, right=np.nan)\n   463→        vis = np.isfinite(pos).all(axis=1)\n   464→        if fill_missing:\n   465→            # nearest-neighbour fill (fillmissing(...,'nearest')) as in findPosition.m\n   466→            if vis.any():\n   467→                idx = np.arange(NFINE)\n   468→                pos = np.stack([np.interp(idx, idx[vis], pos[vis, d]) for d in range(2)], axis=1)\n   469→            else:\n   470→                continue\n   471→        vel = np.gradient(pos, axis=0)\n   472→        if fill_missing:\n   473→            base = np.nanmedian(np.diff(pos, axis=0), axis=0)   # findVelocity.m\n   474→            vel = vel - base[0]\n   475→        sp = np.hypot(vel[:, 0], vel[:, 1])\n   476→        sp[~vis] = np.nan                                       # not visible\n   477→        speed[r] = sp\n   478→        visible[r] = vis\n   479→    return speed, visible\n   480→\n   481→def motion_energy_trials(me, sess, keep_trials, vidshift, align_times):\n   482→    \"\"\"DataLoadingScripts/loadMotionEnergy.m: interpolate 400 Hz ME onto the trial time axis.\"\"\"\n   483→    nkeep = len(keep_trials)\n   484→    out = np.full((nkeep, NFINE), np.nan)\n   485→    if me is None:\n   486→        return out\n   487→    for r, t in enumerate(keep_trials):\n   488→        if t >= len(me['data']):\n   489→            continue\n   490→        y = me['data'][t]\n   491→        ft = frame_times_for_trial(sess, t, vidshift, align_times[t])\n   492→        m = min(ft.size, y.size)\n   493→        if m < 2:\n   494→            continue\n   495→        ft, y = ft[:m], y[:m]\n   496→        valid = np.isfinite(ft) & np.isfinite(y)\n   497→        if valid.sum() < 2:\n   498→            continue\n   499→        v = np.interp(TCENT, ft[valid], y[valid], left=np.nan, right=np.nan)\n   500→        # fillmissing(...,'nearest')\n   501→        ok = np.isfinite(v)\n   502→        if not ok.all() and ok.any():\n   503→            idx = np.arange(NFINE)\n   504→            v = np.interp(idx, idx[ok], v[ok])\n   505→        out[r] = v\n   506→    return out\n   507→\n   508→def discretize(values, nan_class=2):\n   509→    \"\"\"0 = < 50th percentile, 1 = >= 50th percentile (per session), 2 = not visible/no video.\"\"\"\n   510→    finite = np.isfinite(values)\n   511→    cls = np.full(values.shape, nan_class, dtype=np.int64)\n   512→    if finite.any():\n   513→        thr = np.percentile(values[finite], 50)\n   514→        cls[finite & (values >= thr)] = 1\n   515→        cls[finite & (values < thr)] = 0\n   516→    else:\n   517→        thr = np.nan\n   518→    return cls, thr\n   519→\n   520→# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   521→# Per-session conversion\n   522→# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   523→def region_of_probe(sess, probe):\n   524→    \"\"\"Brain region label for a probe, from obj.ex.probe.loc (default ALM: all sessions\n   525→    come from the reference's *_ALMVideo loaders).\"\"\"\n   526→    locs = sess.get('probe_loc', [])\n   527→    if len(locs) >= probe:\n   528→        s = locs[probe - 1].upper()\n   529→        if 'ALM' in s:\n   530→            return 'ALM'\n   531→        if 'M1TJ' in s or 'TJM1' in s:\n   532→            return 'tjM1'\n   533→    return 'ALM'\n   534→\n   535→def process_session(args):\n   536→    anm, date, probes, ddir, task, show = args\n   537→    t0 = time.time()\n   538→    fn = os.path.join(ddir, 'data_structure_%s_%s.mat' % (anm, date))\n   539→    feats = [TONGUE_FEAT] + PAW_FEATS\n   540→    sess = load_session(fn, probes, feats)\n   541→    t_load = time.time() - t0\n   542→\n   543→    bp = sess['bp']\n   544→    N = int(bp['Ntrials'][0])\n   545→    ev = bp['ev']\n   546→    align = ev[ALIGN_EVENT].astype(float)\n   547→    hit = np.nan_to_num(bp['hit']).astype(bool)\n   548→    miss = np.nan_to_num(bp['miss']).astype(bool)\n   549→    no = np.nan_to_num(bp['no']).astype(bool)\n   550→    R = np.nan_to_num(bp['R']).astype(bool)\n   551→    L = np.nan_to_num(bp['L']).astype(bool)\n   552→    early = np.nan_to_num(bp['early']).astype(bool)\n   553→    aw = np.nan_to_num(bp['autowater']).astype(bool)\n   554→    stim = (np.nan_to_num(bp['stim_enable']).astype(bool)\n   555→            if bp['stim_enable'] is not None else np.zeros(N, bool))\n   556→    for a in (hit, miss, no, R, L, early, aw, stim):\n   557→        assert a.size >= N\n   558→\n   559→    # ---- trial curation: reference conditions use ~stim.enable & ~early -------------\n   560→    valid_align = np.isfinite(align[:N])\n   561→    keep = (~stim[:N]) & (~early[:N]) & valid_align\n   562→    keep_trials = np.nonzero(keep)[0]\n   563→    if keep_trials.size < MIN_TRIALS:\n   564→        return dict(ok=False, reason='too few trials', sess='%s_%s' % (anm, date))\n   565→\n   566→    # ---- neural --------------------------------------------------------------------\n   567→    t1 = time.time()\n   568→    rates, ids = bin_spikes(sess['clu'], probes, align, keep_trials, N)\n   569→    t_neural = time.time() - t1\n   570→    if rates.shape[1] == 0:\n   571→        return dict(ok=False, reason='no units', sess='%s_%s' % (anm, date))\n   572→    # Drop trials with no ephys coverage: in a few sessions (e.g. JEB24_2023-10-23) the\n   573→    # SpikeGLX recording ends before the behavioural session, so the trailing trials contain\n   574→    # no spikes from ANY unit (impossible over a 5 s window with tens of simultaneously\n   575→    # recorded units unless the probe was no longer recording).\n   576→    has_spikes = rates.sum(axis=(1, 2)) > 0\n   577→    n_noephys = int((~has_spikes).sum())\n   578→    if n_noephys:\n   579→        rates = rates[has_spikes]\n   580→        keep_trials = keep_trials[has_spikes]\n   581→        if keep_trials.size < MIN_TRIALS:\n   582→            return dict(ok=False, reason='too few trials with ephys', sess='%s_%s' % (anm, date))\n   583→    # removeLowFRClusters.m: mean firing rate across trials & time must exceed lowFR\n   584→    mean_fr = rates.mean(axis=(0, 2))\n   585→    use = mean_fr > LOW_FR\n   586→    rates = rates[:, use, :]\n   587→    ids = [c for c, u in zip(ids, use) if u]\n   588→    if rates.shape[1] < MIN_UNITS:\n   589→        return dict(ok=False, reason='fewer than %d units (%d)' % (MIN_UNITS, rates.shape[1]),\n   590→                    sess='%s_%s' % (anm, date))\n   591→\n   592→    # ---- per-trial task variables ---------------------------------------------------\n   593→    kt = keep_trials\n   594→    lick_dir = np.full(kt.size, 2, dtype=np.int64)          # 2 = none (ignore trials)\n   595→    right_lick = (R[kt] & hit[kt]) | (L[kt] & miss[kt])     # funcs/getPrevChoice.m\n   596→    left_lick = (L[kt] & hit[kt]) | (R[kt] & miss[kt])\n   597→    lick_dir[right_lick] = 1\n   598→    lick_dir[left_lick] = 0\n   599→    context = np.where(aw[kt], 0, 1).astype(np.int64)       # 0 = WC (autowater), 1 = DR\n   600→    outcome = np.full(kt.size, 2, dtype=np.int64)           # 2 = ignore\n   601→    outcome[hit[kt]] = 1\n   602→    outcome[miss[kt]] = 0\n   603→\n   604→    # ---- video-derived outputs ------------------------------------------------------\n   605→    t2 = time.time()\n   606→    vidshift = video_offset(sess)\n   607→    tongue_sp, _ = feature_speed(sess, TONGUE_FEAT, kt, vidshift, align, fill_missing=False)\n   608→    paw_sps = [feature_speed(sess, k, kt, vidshift, align, fill_missing=True)[0] for k in PAW_FEATS]\n   609→    paw_sp = np.nanmean(np.stack(paw_sps, axis=0), axis=0) if paw_sps else np.full_like(tongue_sp, np.nan)\n   610→    mefn = os.path.join(ddir, 'motionEnergy_%s_%s.mat' % (anm, date))\n   611→    me = load_motion_energy(mefn) if os.path.exists(mefn) else None\n   612→    me_fine = motion_energy_trials(me, sess, kt, vidshift, align)\n   613→    t_video = time.time() - t2\n   614→\n   615→    tongue_ds = downsample_mean(tongue_sp, nanmean=True)\n   616→    paw_ds = downsample_mean(paw_sp, nanmean=True)\n   617→    me_ds = downsample_mean(me_fine, nanmean=True)\n   618→    tongue_cls, tongue_thr = discretize(tongue_ds)\n   619→    paw_cls, paw_thr = discretize(paw_ds)\n   620→    me_cls, me_thr = discretize(me_ds)\n   621→\n   622→    # ---- assemble -------------------------------------------------------------------\n   623→    neural = [np.ascontiguousarray(rates[i]).astype(np.float32) for i in range(kt.size)]\n   624→    tin = TOUT.astype(np.float32)[None, :]\n   625→    inputs = [tin.copy() for _ in range(kt.size)]\n   626→    outputs = []\n   627→    for i in range(kt.size):\n   628→        o = np.empty((6, NOUT), dtype=np.int64)\n   629→        o[0] = lick_dir[i]\n   630→        o[1] = context[i]\n   631→        o[2] = outcome[i]\n   632→        o[3] = tongue_cls[i]\n   633→        o[4] = paw_cls[i]\n   634→        o[5] = me_cls[i]\n   635→        outputs.append(o)\n   636→    regions = [region_of_probe(sess, p) for p, _ in ids]\n   637→\n   638→    info = dict(ok=True, sess='%s_%s' % (anm, date), anm=anm, date=date, task=task,\n   639→                probes=probes, ntrials_total=N, ntrials_kept=int(kt.size),\n   640→                nunits_prefilter=int(use.size), nunits=int(rates.shape[1]),\n   641→                nstim=int(stim[:N].sum()), nearly=int(early[:N].sum()), n_noephys=n_noephys,\n   642→                vidshift=float(vidshift), me_thresh_session=(None if me is None else me['moveThresh']),\n   643→                thr=dict(tongue=float(tongue_thr) if np.isfinite(tongue_thr) else None,\n   644→                         paw=float(paw_thr) if np.isfinite(paw_thr) else None,\n   645→                         me=float(me_thr) if np.isfinite(me_thr) else None),\n   646→                frac_hit=float(hit[kt].mean()), frac_wc=float(aw[kt].mean()),\n   647→                timing=dict(load=t_load, neural=t_neural, video=t_video, total=time.time() - t0))\n   648→\n   649→    result = dict(ok=True, neural=neural, input=inputs, output=outputs,\n   650→                  regions=regions, anm=anm, info=info)\n   651→    if show:\n   652→        plot_processing(sess, kt, align, rates, tongue_sp, paw_sp, me_fine,\n   653→                        tongue_ds, paw_ds, me_ds, tongue_cls, paw_cls, me_cls,\n   654→                        lick_dir, context, outcome, '%s_%s' % (anm, date), me)\n   655→    return result\n   656→\n   657→# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   658→# Diagnostic plots (--show-processing)\n   659→# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   660→def plot_processing(sess, kt, align, rates, tongue_sp, paw_sp, me_fine,\n   661→                    tongue_ds, paw_ds, me_ds, tongue_cls, paw_cls, me_cls,\n   662→                    lick_dir, context, outcome, sessname, me):\n   663→    import matplotlib\n   664→    matplotlib.use('Agg')\n   665→    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n   666→    bp = sess['bp']\n   667→    ev = bp['ev']\n   668→    fig, ax = plt.subplots(5, 2, figsize=(16, 18))\n   669→\n   670→    # 1. spike raster + binned rate for one neuron/trial\n   671→    itr = 0\n   672→    tnum = kt[itr]\n   673→    probe = list(sess['clu'].keys())[0]\n   674→    c = sess['clu'][probe]\n   675→    iu = 0\n   676→    for i, q in enumerate(c['quality']):\n   677→        if q.strip().lower() not in BAD_QUALITY:\n   678→            iu = i\n   679→            break\n   680→    trm = c['trial'][iu].astype(int) - 1\n   681→    tm = c['trialtm'][iu]\n   682→    spk = tm[trm == tnum] - align[tnum]\n   683→    ax[0, 0].eventplot([spk], colors='k')\n   684→    ax[0, 0].set_title('%s: aligned spikes, probe %d unit %d, trial %d' % (sessname, probe, iu, tnum))\n   685→    ax[0, 0].set_xlim(TMIN, TMAX); ax[0, 0].axvline(0, color='r')\n   686→    ax[0, 0].set_xlabel('time from go cue (s)')\n   687→\n   688→    ax[0, 1].plot(TOUT, rates[itr].mean(axis=0), 'k')\n   689→    ax[0, 1].axvline(0, color='r')\n   690→    ax[0, 1].set_title('population mean rate (spk/s), trial %d, %d units' % (tnum, rates.shape[1]))\n   691→    ax[0, 1].set_xlabel('time from go cue (s)')\n   692→\n   693→    # 2. PSTH by lick direction (sanity: choice selectivity)\n   694→    for lab, name, col in [(1, 'right', 'b'), (0, 'left', 'r')]:\n   695→        m = lick_dir == lab\n   696→        if m.sum() > 2:\n   697→            ax[1, 0].plot(TOUT, rates[m].mean(axis=(0, 1)), col, label='%s (n=%d)' % (name, m.sum()))\n   698→    ax[1, 0].legend(); ax[1, 0].axvline(0, color='k', ls='--')\n   699→    ax[1, 0].set_title('population PSTH by lick direction'); ax[1, 0].set_xlabel('time from go cue (s)')\n   700→\n   701→    # 3. lick raster vs tongue visibility\n   702→    nshow = min(40, kt.size)\n   703→    for r in range(nshow):\n   704→        t = kt[r]\n   705→        for arr, col in [(ev['lickR'][t], 'b'), (ev['lickL'][t], 'r')]:\n   706→            if arr.size:\n   707→                x = arr - align[t]\n   708→                x = x[(x > TMIN) & (x < TMAX)]\n   709→                ax[1, 1].plot(x, np.full(x.size, r), col + '.', ms=3)\n   710→        vis = np.isfinite(tongue_sp[r])\n   711→        ax[1, 1].plot(TCENT[vis], np.full(vis.sum(), r), 'g.', ms=1, alpha=0.3)\n   712→    ax[1, 1].axvline(0, color='k')\n   713→    ax[1, 1].set_title('lick times (blue=R, red=L) and tongue visible (green)')\n   714→    ax[1, 1].set_xlabel('time from go cue (s)'); ax[1, 1].set_xlim(TMIN, TMAX)\n   715→\n   716→    # 4. tongue speed: fine vs binned vs discretized\n   717→    ax[2, 0].plot(TCENT, tongue_sp[itr], 'k.-', ms=2, label='fine (10 ms)')\n   718→    ax[2, 0].plot(TOUT, tongue_ds[itr], 'c.-', label='binned (50 ms)')\n   719→    ax[2, 0].axvline(0, color='r')\n   720→    ax[2, 0].legend(); ax[2, 0].set_title('tongue speed, trial %d (NaN = not visible)' % tnum)\n   721→    ax[2, 1].imshow(tongue_cls[:nshow], aspect='auto', interpolation='nearest',\n   722→                    extent=[TOUT[0], TOUT[-1], nshow, 0], vmin=0, vmax=2)\n   723→    ax[2, 1].set_title('tongue_velocity class (0 slow, 1 fast, 2 not visible)')\n   724→\n   725→    # 5. paw + ME\n   726→    ax[3, 0].plot(TCENT, paw_sp[itr], 'k', label='paw speed (fine)')\n   727→    ax[3, 0].plot(TOUT, paw_ds[itr], 'c.-', label='binned')\n   728→    ax[3, 0].axvline(0, color='r'); ax[3, 0].legend(); ax[3, 0].set_title('paw speed, trial %d' % tnum)\n   729→    ax[3, 1].imshow(paw_cls[:nshow], aspect='auto', interpolation='nearest',\n   730→                    extent=[TOUT[0], TOUT[-1], nshow, 0], vmin=0, vmax=2)\n   731→    ax[3, 1].set_title('paw_velocity class')\n   732→\n   733→    ax[4, 0].plot(TCENT, me_fine[itr], 'k', label='motion energy (fine)')\n   734→    ax[4, 0].plot(TOUT, me_ds[itr], 'c.-', label='binned')\n   735→    if me is not None:\n   736→        ax[4, 0].axhline(me['moveThresh'], color='m', ls=':', label='session moveThresh')\n   737→    ax[4, 0].axvline(0, color='r'); ax[4, 0].legend(); ax[4, 0].set_title('motion energy, trial %d' % tnum)\n   738→    ax[4, 1].imshow(me_cls[:nshow], aspect='auto', interpolation='nearest',\n   739→                    extent=[TOUT[0], TOUT[-1], nshow, 0], vmin=0, vmax=2)\n   740→    ax[4, 1].set_title('motion_energy class')\n   741→    for a in ax.ravel():\n   742→        a.set_xlabel('time from go cue (s)')\n   743→    fig.suptitle('Processing steps: %s  (outputs: lickdir %s, context %s, outcome %s)'\n   744→                 % (sessname, np.bincount(lick_dir, minlength=3),\n   745→                    np.bincount(context, minlength=2), np.bincount(outcome, minlength=3)))\n   746→    fig.tight_layout()\n   747→    fig.savefig('processing_%s.png' % sessname, dpi=110)\n   748→    plt.close(fig)\n   749→\n   750→    # second figure: event-time sanity (sample / delay / go cue) and trial-average traces\n   751→    fig, ax = plt.subplots(2, 1, figsize=(10, 8))\n   752→    smp = ev['sample'][kt] - align[kt]\n   753→    dly = ev['delay'][kt] - align[kt]\n   754→    ax[0].plot(smp, np.arange(kt.size), 'g.', ms=3, label='sample onset')\n   755→    ax[0].plot(dly, np.arange(kt.size), 'b.', ms=3, label='delay onset')\n   756→    ax[0].axvline(0, color='r', label='go cue (alignment)')\n   757→    ax[0].set_xlim(TMIN, TMAX); ax[0].legend(); ax[0].set_xlabel('time from go cue (s)')\n   758→    ax[0].set_ylabel('trial'); ax[0].set_title('event times relative to alignment event')\n   759→    ax[1].plot(TOUT, np.nanmean(me_ds, axis=0), 'k', label='mean motion energy')\n   760→    ax[1].plot(TOUT, 100 * np.mean(tongue_cls != 2, axis=0), 'g', label='% trials tongue visible')\n   761→    ax[1].axvline(0, color='r'); ax[1].legend(); ax[1].set_xlabel('time from go cue (s)')\n   762→    ax[1].set_title('trial-averaged movement signals')\n   763→    fig.tight_layout()\n   764→    fig.savefig('processing_%s_alignment.png' % sessname, dpi=110)\n   765→    plt.close(fig)\n   766→\n   767→# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   768→# Main\n   769→# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   770→def main():\n   771→    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)\n   772→    ap.add_argument('outfile')\n   773→    ap.add_argument('--full', action='store_true', default=True)\n   774→    ap.add_argument('--sample', action='store_true')\n   775→    ap.add_argument('--show-processing', action='store_true')\n   776→    ap.add_argument('--nproc', type=int, default=12)\n   777→    args = ap.parse_args()\n   778→\n   779→    sessions = SESSIONS[:2] if args.sample else SESSIONS\n   780→    show = args.show_processing\n   781→    jobs = [(a, d, p, dd, tk, show and i < 2) for i, (a, d, p, dd, tk) in enumerate(sessions)]\n   782→\n   783→    print('Converting %d sessions (%s mode)' % (len(jobs), 'sample' if args.sample else 'full'))\n   784→    t0 = time.time()\n   785→    if args.nproc > 1 and len(jobs) > 1:\n   786→        with Pool(min(args.nproc, len(jobs))) as pool:\n   787→            results = pool.map(process_session, jobs)\n   788→    else:\n   789→        results = [process_session(j) for j in jobs]\n   790→    print('All sessions processed in %.1f s' % (time.time() - t0))\n   791→\n   792→    data = dict(neural=[], input=[], output=[], subjects=[], subject_idx=[],\n   793→                brain_regions=['ALM', 'tjM1'], brain_region_idx=[])\n   794→    session_info = []\n   795→    for r in results:\n   796→        if not r.get('ok'):\n   797→            print('  EXCLUDED %s: %s' % (r['sess'], r['reason']))\n   798→            continue\n   799→        info = r['info']\n   800→        print('  %s (%s): %d units, %d/%d trials, load %.1fs neural %.1fs video %.1fs total %.1fs'\n   801→              % (info['sess'], info['task'], info['nunits'], info['ntrials_kept'],\n   802→                 info['ntrials_total'], info['timing']['load'], info['timing']['neural'],\n   803→                 info['timing']['video'], info['timing']['total']))\n   804→        if r['anm'] not in data['subjects']:\n   805→            data['subjects'].append(r['anm'])\n   806→        data['subject_idx'].append(data['subjects'].index(r['anm']))\n   807→        data['neural'].append(r['neural'])\n   808→        data['input'].append(r['input'])\n   809→        data['output'].append(r['output'])\n   810→        data['brain_region_idx'].append(\n   811→            np.array([data['brain_regions'].index(x) for x in r['regions']], dtype=np.int64))\n   812→        session_info.append(info)\n   813→\n   814→    data['subject_idx'] = np.array(data['subject_idx'], dtype=np.int64)\n   815→    data['input_names'] = ['time_from_go_cue']\n   816→    data['output_names'] = ['lick_direction', 'context', 'outcome',\n   817→                            'tongue_velocity', 'paw_velocity', 'motion_energy']\n   818→    data['output_values'] = [\n   819→        ['left', 'right', 'none'],\n   820→        ['WC', 'DR'],\n   821→        ['incorrect', 'correct', 'ignore'],\n   822→        ['below_median', 'above_median', 'not_visible'],\n   823→        ['below_median', 'above_median', 'not_visible'],\n   824→        ['below_median', 'above_median', 'no_video'],\n   825→    ]\n   826→    data['metadata'] = dict(\n   827→        task_description=(\n   828→            'Head-fixed mice alternate block-wise between a delayed-response (DR) task '\n   829→            '(auditory sample tone instructs left/right lick direction, delay epoch, auditory go cue) '\n   830→            'and a water-cued (WC) task (water delivered at a random time at a random port, no auditory cues). '\n   831→            'Neural activity is single-unit spiking recorded with Neuropixels/H2 probes in ALM (and tjM1 on a '\n   832→            'few dual-probe sessions). --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndef gausswin(N, alpha=2.5):\n    \"\"\"MATLAB gausswin(N, alpha) (default alpha = 2.5).\"\"\"\n    n = np.arange(N) - (N - 1) / 2.0\n    return np.exp(-0.5 * (alpha * n / ((N - 1) / 2.0)) ** 2)\n\ndef causal_kernel(N=SMOOTH_N):\n    \"\"\"Kernel used by utils/mySmooth.m: gausswin with the first --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndef bin_spikes(clu, probes, align_times, keep_trials, ntrials):\n    \"\"\"Bin + smooth spikes exactly like            counts = np.zeros((nkeep, NFINE), dtype=np.float32)\n            if tr.size:\n                sel = ok & np.isfinite(tm)\n                if sel.any():\n                    aligned = tm[sel] - align_times[tr[sel]]\n                    b = np.floor((aligned - TMIN) / DT_FINE).astype(np.int64)\n                    inwin = (b >= 0) & (b < NFINE)\n                    if inwin.any():\n                        flat = row[sel][inwin] * NFINE + b[inwin]\n                        np.add.at(counts.reshape(-1), flat, 1.0)\n            rate = counts / DT_FINE                                # spikes / s\n            rate = my_smooth(rate)                                 # causal gaussian\n            rates.append(downsample_mean(rate).astype(np.float32))\n            ids.append((p, i))\n    if not rates:\n        return np.zeros((nkeep, 0, NOUT), np.float32), []\n    return np.stack(rates, axis=1), ids\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Kinematics / motion energy\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndef feature_speed(sess, key, keep_trials, vidshift, align_times, fill_missing):\n    \"\"\"Speed of a DLC feature on the fine time axis, NaN where the feature is not visible.\n\n    Mirrors funcs/kinematics/findPosition.m + findVelocity.m:\n      * interp1(frameTimes - vidshift - alignEvent, [x y], taxis)\n      * tongue: no smoothing, NaNs kept (not visible)\n      * other features: nearest-fill + baseline-derivative subtraction\n    \"\"\"\n    tr = sess['traj'].get(key)\n    nkeep = len(keep_trials)\n    speed = np.full((nkeep, NFINE), np.nan)\n    visible = np.zeros((nkeep, NFINE), bool)\n    if tr is None:\n        return speed, visible\n    nd = tr['NdroppedFrames']\n    for r, t in enumerate(keep_trials):\n        if t >= len(tr['xy']):\n            continue\n        if t < len(nd) and np.isnan(nd[t]):      # reference skips these trials\n            continue\n        ft = frame_times_for_trial(sess, t, vidshift, align_times[t])\n        xy = tr['xy'][t]\n        if ft.size != xy.shape[0] or ft.size < 2:\n            m = min(ft.size, xy.shape[0])\n            if m < 2:\n                continue\n            ft, xy = ft[:m], xy[:m]\n        valid = np.isfinite(ft)\n        if valid.sum() < 2:\n            continue\n        pos = np.empty((NFINE, 2))\n        for d in range(2):\n            pos[:, d] = np.interp(TCENT, ft[valid], xy[valid, d], left=np.nan, right=np.nan)\n        vis = np.isfinite(pos).all(axis=1)\n        if fill_missing:\n            # nearest-neighbour fill (fillmissing(...,'nearest')) as in findPosition.m\n            if vis.any():\n                idx = np.arange(NFINE)\n                pos = np.stack([np.interp(idx, idx[vis], pos[vis, d]) for d in range(2)], axis=1)\n            else:\n                continue\n        vel = np.gradient(pos, axis=0)\n        if fill_missing:\n            base = np.nanmedian(np.diff(pos, axis=0), axis=0)   # findVelocity.m\n            vel = vel - base[0]\n        sp = np.hypot(vel[:, 0], vel[:, 1])\n        sp[~vis] = np.nan                                       # not visible\n        speed[r] = sp\n        visible[r] = vis\n    return speed, visible\n\ndef motion_energy_trials(me, sess, keep_trials, vidshift, align_times):\n    \"\"\"DataLoadingScripts/loadMotionEnergy.m: interpolate 400 Hz ME onto the trial time axis.\"\"\"\n    nkeep = len(keep_trials)\n    out = np.full((nkeep, NFINE), np.nan)\n    if me is None:\n        return out\n    for r, t in enumerate(keep_trials):\n        if t >= len(me['data']):\n            continue\n        y = me['data'][t]\n        ft = frame_times_for_trial(sess, t, vidshift, align_times[t])\n        m = min(ft.size, y.size)\n        if m < 2:\n            continue\n        ft, y = ft[:m], y[:m]\n        valid = np.isfinite(ft) & np.isfinite(y)\n        if valid.sum() < 2:\n            continue\n        v = np.interp(TCENT, ft[valid], y[valid], left=np.nan, right=np.nan)\n        # fillmissing(...,'nearest')\n        ok = np.isfinite(v)\n        if not ok.all() and ok.any():\n            idx = np.arange(NFINE)\n            v = np.interp(idx, idx[ok], v[ok])\n        out[r] = v\n    return out\n\ndef discretize(values, nan_class=2):\n    \"\"\"0 = < 50th percentile, 1 = >= 50th percentile (per session), 2 = not visible/no video.\"\"\"\n    finite = np.isfinite(values)\n    cls = np.full(values.shape, nan_class, dtype=np.int64)\n    if finite.any():\n        thr = np.percentile(values[finite], 50)\n        cls[finite & (values >= thr)] = 1\n        cls[finite & (values < thr)] = 0\n    else:\n        thr = np.nan\n    return cls, thr\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Per-session conversion\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndef region_of_probe(sess, probe):\n    \"\"\"Brain region label for a probe, from obj.ex.probe.loc (default ALM: all sessions\n    come from the reference's *_ALMVideo loaders).\"\"\"\n    locs = sess.get('probe_loc', [])\n    if len(locs) >= probe:\n        s = locs[probe - 1].upper()\n        if 'ALM' in s:\n            return 'ALM'\n        if 'M1TJ' in s or 'TJM1' in s:\n            return 'tjM1'\n    return 'ALM'\n\ndef process_session(args):\n    anm, date, probes, ddir, task, show = args\n    t0 = time.time()\n    fn = os.path.join(ddir, 'data_structure_%s_%s.mat' % (anm, date))\n    feats = [TONGUE_FEAT] + PAW_FEATS\n    sess = load_session(fn, probes, feats)\n    t_load = time.time() - t0\n\n    bp = sess['bp']\n    N = int(bp['Ntrials'][0])\n    ev = bp['ev']\n    align = ev[ALIGN_EVENT].astype(float)\n    hit = np.nan_to_num(bp['hit']).astype(bool)\n    miss = np.nan_to_num(bp['miss']).astype(bool)\n    no = np.nan_to_num(bp['no']).astype(bool)\n    R = np.nan_to_num(bp['R']).astype(bool)\n    L = np.nan_to_num(bp['L']).astype(bool)\n    early = np.nan_to_num(bp['early']).astype(bool)\n    aw = np.nan_to_num(bp['autowater']).astype(bool)\n    stim = (np.nan_to_num(bp['stim_enable']).astype(bool)\n            if bp['stim_enable'] is not None else np.zeros(N, bool))\n    for a in (hit, miss, no, R, L, early, aw, stim):\n        assert a.size >= N\n\n    # ---- trial curation: reference conditions use ~stim.enable & ~early -------------\n    valid_align = np.isfinite(align[:N])\n    keep = (~stim[:N]) & (~early[:N]) & valid_align\n    keep_trials = np.nonzero(keep)[0]\n    if keep_trials.size < MIN_TRIALS:\n        return dict(ok=False, reason='too few trials', sess='%s_%s' % (anm, date))\n\n    # ---- neural --------------------------------------------------------------------\n    t1 = time.time()\n    rates, ids = bin_spikes(sess['clu'], probes, align, keep_trials, N)\n    t_neural = time.time() - t1\n    if rates.shape[1] == 0:\n        return dict(ok=False, reason='no units', sess='%s_%s' % (anm, date))\n    # Drop trials with no ephys coverage: in a few sessions (e.g. JEB24_2023-10-23) the\n    # SpikeGLX recording ends before the behavioural session, so the trailing trials contain\n    # no spikes from ANY unit (impossible over a 5 s window with tens of simultaneously\n    # recorded units unless the probe was no longer recording).\n    has_spikes = rates.sum(axis=(1, 2)) > 0\n    n_noephys = int((~has_spikes).sum())\n    if n_noephys:\n        rates = rates[has_spikes]\n        keep_trials = keep_trials[has_spikes]\n        if keep_trials.size < MIN_TRIALS:\n            return dict(ok=False, reason='too few trials with ephys', sess='%s_%s' % (anm, date))\n    # removeLowFRClusters.m: mean firing rate across trials & time must exceed lowFR\n    mean_fr = rates.mean(axis=(0, 2))\n    use = mean_fr > LOW_FR\n    rates = rates[:, use, :]\n    ids = [c for c, u in zip(ids, use) if u]\n    if rates.shape[1] < MIN_UNITS:\n        return dict(ok=False, reason='fewer than %d units (%d)' % (MIN_UNITS, rates.shape[1]),\n                    sess='%s_%s' % (anm, date))\n\n    # ---- per-trial task variables ---------------------------------------------------\n    kt = keep_trials\n    lick_dir = np.full(kt.size, 2, dtype=np.int64)          # 2 = none (ignore trials)\n    right_lick = (R[kt] & hit[kt]) | (L[kt] & miss[kt])     # funcs/getPrevChoice.m\n    left_lick = (L[kt] & hit[kt]) | (R[kt] & miss[kt])\n    lick_dir[right_lick] = 1\n    lick_dir[left_lick] = 0\n    context = np.where(aw[kt], 0, 1).astype(np.int64)       # 0 = WC (autowater), 1 = DR\n    outcome = np.full(kt.size, 2, dtype=np.int64)           # 2 = ignore\n    outcome[hit[kt]] = 1\n    outcome[miss[kt]] = 0\n\n    # ---- video-derived outputs ------------------------------------------------------\n    t2 = time.time()\n    vidshift = video_offset(sess)\n    tongue_sp, _ = feature_speed(sess, TONGUE_FEAT, kt, vidshift, align, fill_missing=False)\n    paw_sps = [feature_speed(sess, k, kt, vidshift, align, fill_missing=True)[0] for k in PAW_FEATS]\n    paw_sp = np.nanmean(np.stack(paw_sps, axis=0), axis=0) if paw_sps else np.full_like(tongue_sp, np.nan)\n    mefn = os.path.join(ddir, 'motionEnergy_%s_%s.mat' % (anm, date))\n    me = load_motion_energy(mefn) if os.path.exists(mefn) else None\n    me_fine = motion_energy_trials(me, sess, kt, vidshift, align)\n    t_video = time.time() - t2\n\n    tongue_ds = downsample_mean(tongue_sp, nanmean=True)\n    paw_ds = downsample_mean(paw_sp, nanmean=True)\n    me_ds = downsample_mean(me_fine, nanmean=True)\n    tongue_cls, tongue_thr = discretize(tongue_ds)\n    paw_cls, paw_thr = discretize(paw_ds)\n    me_cls, me_thr = discretize(me_ds)\n\n    # ---- assemble -------------------------------------------------------------------\n    neural = [np.ascontiguousarray(rates[i]).astype(np.float32) for i in range(kt.size)]\n    tin = TOUT.astype(np.float32)[None, :]\n    inputs = [tin.copy() for _ in range(kt.size)]\n    outputs = []\n    for i in range(kt.size):\n        o = np.empty((6, NOUT), dtype=np.int64)\n        o[0] = lick_dir[i]\n        o[1] = context[i]\n        o[2] = outcome[i]\n        o[3] = tongue_cls[i]\n        o[4] = paw_cls[i]\n        o[5] = me_cls[i]\n        outputs.append(o)\n    regions = [region_of_probe(sess, p) for p, _ in ids]\n\n    info = dict(ok=True, sess='%s_%s' % (anm, date), anm=anm, date=date, task=task,\n                probes=probes, ntrials_total=N, ntrials_kept=int(kt.size),\n                nunits_prefilter=int(use.size), nunits=int(rates.shape[1]),\n                nstim=int(stim[:N].sum()), nearly=int(early[:N].sum()), n_noephys=n_noephys,\n                vidshift=float(vidshift), me_thresh_session=(None if me is None else me['moveThresh']),\n                thr=dict(tongue=float(tongue_thr) if np.isfinite(tongue_thr) else None,\n                         paw=float(paw_thr) if np.isfinite(paw_thr) else None,\n                         me=float(me_thr) if np.isfinite(me_thr) else None),\n                frac_hit=float(hit[kt].mean()), frac_wc=float(aw[kt].mean()),\n                timing=dict(load=t_load, neural=t_neural, video=t_video, total=time.time() - t0))\n\n    result = dict(ok=True, neural=neural, input=inputs, output=outputs,\n                  regions=regions, anm=anm, info=info)\n    if show:\n        plot_processing(sess, kt, align, rates, tongue_sp, paw_sp, me_fine,\n                        tongue_ds, paw_ds, me_ds, tongue_cls, paw_cls, me_cls,\n                        lick_dir, context, outcome, '%s_%s' % (anm, date), me)\n    return result\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Diagnostic plots (--show-processing)\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndef plot_processing(sess, kt, align, rates, tongue_sp, paw_sp, me_fine,\n                    tongue_ds, paw_ds, me_ds, tongue_cls, paw_cls, me_cls,\n                    lick_dir, context, outcome, sessname, me):\n    import matplotlib\n    matplotlib.use('Agg')\n    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n    bp = sess['bp']\n    ev = bp['ev']\n    fig, ax = plt.subplots(5, 2, figsize=(16, 18))\n\n    # 1. spike raster + binned rate for one neuron/trial\n    itr = 0\n    tnum = kt[itr]\n    probe = list(sess['clu'].keys())[0]\n    c = sess['clu'][probe]\n    iu = 0\n    for i, q in enumerate(c['quality']):\n        if q.strip().lower() not in BAD_QUALITY:\n            iu = i\n            break\n    trm = c['trial'][iu].astype(int) - 1\n    tm = c['trialtm'][iu]\n    spk = tm[trm == tnum] - align[tnum]\n    ax[0, 0].eventplot([spk], colors='k')\n    ax[0, 0].set_title('%s: aligned spikes, 1].plot(TOUT, rates[itr].mean(axis=0), 'k')\n    ax[0, 1].axvline(0, color='r')\n    ax[0, 1].set_title('population mean rate (spk/s), trial %d, %d units' % (tnum, rates.shape[1]))\n    ax[0, 1].set_xlabel('time from go cue (s)')\n\n    # 2. PSTH by lick direction (sanity: choice selectivity)\n    for lab, name, col in [(1, 'right', 'b'), (0, 'left', 'r')]:\n        m = lick_dir == lab\n        if m.sum() > 2:\n            ax[1, 0].plot(TOUT, rates[m].mean(axis=(0, 1)), col, label='%s (n=%d)' % (name, m.sum()))\n    ax[1, 0].legend(); ax[1, 0].axvline(0, color='k', ls='--')\n    ax[1, 0].set_title('population PSTH by lick direction'); ax[1, 0].set_xlabel('time from go cue (s)')\n\n    # 3. lick raster vs tongue visibility\n    nshow = min(40, kt.size)\n    for r in range(nshow):\n        t = kt[r]\n        for arr, col in [(ev['lickR'][t], 'b'), (ev['lickL'][t], 'r')]:\n            if arr.size:\n                x = arr - align[t]\n                x = x[(x > TMIN) & (x < TMAX)]\n                ax[1, 1].plot(x, np.full(x.size, r), col + '.', ms=3)\n        vis = np.isfinite(tongue_sp[r])\n        ax[1, 1].plot(TCENT[vis], np.full(vis.sum(), r), 'g.', ms=1, alpha=0.3)\n    ax[1, 1].axvline(0, color='k')\n    ax[1, 1].set_title('lick times (blue=R, red=L) and tongue visible (green)')\n    ax[1, 1].set_xlabel('time from go cue (s)'); ax[1, 1].set_xlim(TMIN, TMAX)\n\n    # 4. tongue speed: fine vs binned vs discretized\n    ax[2, 0].plot(TCENT, tongue_sp[itr], 'k.-', ms=2, label='fine (10 ms)')\n    ax[2, 0].plot(TOUT, tongue_ds[itr], 'c.-', label='binned (50 ms)')\n    ax[2, 0].axvline(0, color='r')\n    ax[2, 0].legend(); ax[2, 0].set_title('tongue speed, trial %d (NaN = not visible)' % tnum)\n    ax[2, 1].imshow(tongue_cls[:nshow], aspect='auto', interpolation='nearest',\n                    extent=[TOUT[0], TOUT[-1], nshow, 0], vmin=0, vmax=2)\n    ax[2, 1].set_title('tongue_velocity class (0 slow, 1 fast, 2 not visible)')\n\n    # 5. paw + ME\n    ax[3, 0].plot(TCENT, paw_sp[itr], 'k', ax[3, 1].imshow(paw_cls[:nshow], aspect='auto', interpolation='nearest',\n                    extent=[TOUT[0], TOUT[-1], nshow, 0], vmin=0, vmax=2)\n    ax[3, 1].set_title('paw_velocity class')\n\n    ax[4, 0].plot(TCENT, me_fine[itr], 'k', label='motion energy (fine)')\n    ax[4, 0].plot(TOUT, me_ds[itr], 'c.-', label='binned')\n    if me is not None:\n        ax[4, 0].axhline(me['moveThresh'],
